      • VIctory has had no commit since 00:57 UTC.

    189. 03:13 24 ноя

      GTA6-BootTest joins the standings

      • GTA6-BootTest, a project this page had missed, boots the game and draws its first interface screen through translated shaders. It joins at stage 5, in sixth place.
      • five-star-recomp now runs for three minutes without a crash, with real images on screen, while the game is still loading.
      • A community project list reports that a second, smaller project, americas-recomp, went offline on 23 November shortly before Leonida. No cause is given for either.

    190. 02:35 24 ноя

      Xbox2Windows reports a visible intro

      • Xbox2Windows: a contributor reports a visible intro video on an RX 9070 XT, with wrong colours and black frames after it. The project moves from stage 3 to stage 5, in sixth place, and has not yet checked a frame against a reference.
      • five-star-recomp is past the assert that stopped it and now streams the game's next stage, compressed textures included. It stops at one texture layout method it has not implemented yet.
      • Six projects now show a picture from the game. None has drawn the world.

    191. 01:58 24 ноя

      Estimate moves back a day

      • The best estimate to playable moves from about 3 days to about 4, around Sat 28 Nov. Nobody has drawn the world in the three and a half hours since the first project got inside it, and frame times there are ten times or more too slow.
      • Palmwave now loads the world at 5 to 30 frames a second with memory levelling off. It stops as the world starts to draw, when the game's watchdog ends a wait for pipelines still compiling.
      • five-star-recomp added a disk pipeline cache. VIctory and XCL's main branch have had no substantive change for about two hours.
      • Press coverage has started: outlets report the game reaching its settings and legal screens on PC, with nothing playable.

    192. 01:38 24 ноя

      Two of Leonida's forks are gone

      • Two of the four contributor forks of Leonida that this page watches stopped answering between 00:19 and 01:37 UTC. The cause is not known, and no takedown notice naming the project has been published.
      • five-star-recomp's first image now shows in the right colours after a fix to 61 pipelines.
      • No project changed stage.

    193. 01:19 24 ноя

      five-star-recomp draws its first picture

      • five-star-recomp: the game's interface layer and intro video now draw, its first image after hours of black frames. It moves to stage 5 and fourth place.
      • An assert inside the game still stops it shortly after, and one drawing fix is being merged.
      • XCL merged a change that skips uploading textures whose memory has not changed. Its status line is unchanged.
      • Five projects now show a picture from the game. None has drawn the world.

    194. 01:00 24 ноя

      Three more projects report start-up progress

      • Palmwave, a fork of XCL, measures a retail game it does not name into world loading on an RTX 5080: 0 to 2 frames a second, with memory growing until the run is stopped at 140 seconds. It joins the standings in third.
      • Its own fix lifts the intro video from 3.5 to about 19 frames a second. It reports that compressed world textures are not decoded yet in this code.
      • Xbox2Windows says local runs of the main executable now reach graphics pipeline creation, its first report of running the title's own code. No rendered frame is verified.
      • Holy-Interoperability presents its first frame of a retail title it does not name, then stops in start-up.

    195. 00:39 24 ноя

      XCL's code is measured on a second PC

      • Palmwave, now built on XCL's code, measured a retail game it does not name on an RTX 5080. It shows first screens at up to 30 frames a second, slows to about 3 frames a second, then crashes as memory grows while the world loads.
      • Its list of what separates that code from playable: memory growth in world loading, which XCL has since fixed, texture decoding failures, refused mesh pipelines and a slow phase with no known cause.
      • TuxBox: shader preparation now compiles 2,755 of 11,429 requests captured from its runs. Its picture is unchanged at the player-selection prompt.
      • VIctory merged 11 fixes from outside contributors in half an hour. Its status is unchanged.

    196. 00:19 24 ноя

      VIctory draws the first mission's title card

      • VIctory: after the world loads, the first mission's title card now renders. It is the first picture past the loading screen that any project on this page has reported.
      • The scene after the card is still dark, at 0.5 to 1.8 seconds a frame, and runs end on a graphics fault within about 55 seconds.
      • five-star-recomp: the game now reads input, starts its save system and pairs a gamepad after a scripted button press. Frames are still black, and an assert inside the game stops it there.
      • Palmwave has switched to XCL's code. XCL's own status line is unchanged, and Leonida is still unreachable.

    197. 23:56 23 ноя

      Leonida halts: its lead steps away and its repository is gone

      • Leonida's lead posted a decision to step away. Its GitHub repository no longer exists at either address, its own mirror server is down, and its chat is reported gone.
      • Whether a takedown was involved is not known. GitHub shows a notice page for repositories removed on a copyright complaint and has published none; contributors' forks and the lead's other repository are still up.
      • The projects ahead of it are unaffected: VIctory, XCL, TuxBox and five-star-recomp were all reachable at 23:56 UTC.

    198. 23:54 23 ноя

      Leonida's GitHub repository is unreachable again

      • Leonida: its GitHub repository stopped answering between 23:31 and 23:48 UTC, at both its new address and its old one. The cause is not known. The earlier gap tonight was a move, and contributors' forks, the owner's other repository and the project's own mirror are all still up. No takedown notice is published.
      • VIctory: two more outside fixes merged. The world is still black at 1.5 to 3 seconds a frame.

    199. 23:31 23 ноя

      VIctory keeps the game world running, still black

      • VIctory: a 23:13 UTC commit fixes the graphics-device failure. The world ran to a four-minute limit. The picture is black, at 1.5 to 3 seconds a frame.
      • By its notes, most of the world's geometry is drawn through a path that is switched off for now, and hundreds of draws a frame have no translated shader.
      • XCL: merged another batch, including the intro-video fix. Its status is unchanged.

    200. 23:14 23 ноя

      XCL merges its world-loading fix

      • XCL: the fix for storage requests that failed while the world loads is on the main branch since 22:54 UTC. No picture of the world is reported yet.
      • VIctory: a contributor's change gets the game past start-up on Intel processors, tested on a second machine. Its status is unchanged: the world loads and the picture is black.
      • Leonida: a pull request reports a repeating render loop that tries to present frames, and says visible frames are not verified.

    201. 22:37 23 ноя

      • XCL: a contributor's pull request fixes most of the storage requests that failed while the world loads. In the contributor's 170-second run 16,384 requests now land and 2,227 still fail. Another pull request fixes the green screen where the intro video plays. Neither is merged.
      • VIctory: no commits since 22:15 UTC. Leonida: merging pull requests again at its new address.

    202. 22:34 23 ноя

      A new leader: VIctory loads the game world, with a black picture

      • VIctory, a one-person project published today, reports the game running through its first-boot screens, intro video and menus at 30 frames a second, then loading the world in about 50 seconds. In the world the picture is black and the graphics device fails within seconds. Tested on one machine.
      • It stays at stage 5: stage 6 needs a picture of the world and player control.
      • Correction on Leonida: its repository was not taken down. The maintainer moved it to a GitHub organisation at about 22:10 UTC, and the old address stopped answering for a few minutes. Pull requests are being merged again.
      • This page was redesigned at 22:33 UTC.

    203. 22:17 23 ноя

      Leonida's GitHub repository goes dark; XCL reaches the settings screens

      • Leonida: the GitHub repository answered at 22:08 UTC and no longer did at 22:12. It now responds like a repository that is missing or private. The cause is not known. Several contributors' forks, the owner's other repository and every other project on this page were still reachable at 22:16 UTC, and GitHub has published no notice.
      • Leonida's own mirror is still up with the same history, last commit 21:01 UTC. This page now reads Leonida from there.
      • XCL: the main branch goes past the first menu into the settings screens at a steady 30 frames per second, with memory use down to about 15 GB. Its status line says gameplay has not been reached.
      • Forecast unchanged for now. A confirmed takedown would push it out.

    204. 21:50 23 ноя

      No change at the front

      • XCL: main branch unchanged since 21:30 UTC. In chat, the contributor who reached the menus says world loading stalls because most storage requests fail. No pull request for that yet.
      • PercyScarlett fixed an early device removal. five-star-recomp ruled out network access as the reason the game idles. TuxBox and OpenScarlett edged forward. None reports a picture.

    205. 21:37 23 ноя

      Chat report: an XCL build gets through the menus and starts loading the game

      • XCL: the contributor behind the first-menus pull request said in the project's chat at 21:36 UTC that the contributor's build now saves preferences, selects a player and begins loading into the main game. The contributor's earlier menu report was confirmed on the main branch within about half an hour.
      • This is a chat message, not a commit or pull request, and it does not claim a frame of the game world or player control. XCL stays at stage 5.
      • Forecast: the in-game step is brought forward to about a day. The playable date is unchanged.

    206. 21:31 23 ноя

      A sixth project presents frames; no new milestone at the front

      • PercyScarlett: reaches the game's frame loop and first present with black frames, then a GPU hang about 5 seconds in. Added to the standings at stage 4.
      • XCL: about 35 more commits merged, including a fix for the menus' missing body text. Its status is unchanged: first menu at 30 frames per second, nothing checked past it. A pull request for the mesh pipelines its minimap needs is open.
      • five-star-recomp: now reads its black frames as the game's own fade from black while it waits on something.
      • OpenScarlett: two more boot obstacles cleared and more than 360 frames at 4K without a crash on a 4 GB graphics card. No picture described.

    207. 21:11 23 ноя

      No new milestone; XCL merges fast behind its first menu

      • XCL: eleven pull requests merged at 20:49 to 20:50 UTC and review fixes at 20:56. Two new pull requests go at the known gaps: one for the missing menu text, one for the mesh-shader slot that the main branch refuses. Neither is merged.
      • Leonida: a correction. Its README now lists the project's own server as a mirror of the repository, so this is a mirror for now, not a move. Both copies match.
      • five-star-recomp, OpenScarlett and TuxBox: no change. Palmwave no longer names the game in its README.

    208. 20:50 23 ноя

      XCL's main branch shows the first menu at 30 frames per second

      • XCL: a 20:47 UTC commit by the maintainer puts the first menu on the main branch. The account prompt is visible at a steady 30 frames per second with no crash, on a 16 GB graphics card. It uses parts of the earlier pull request and its own memory handling. Nothing past that menu has been checked on main.
      • five-star-recomp: start-up loading completes and the game then idles on an early screen, still black.
      • OpenScarlett: reports the first frame's three draws executing and boot moving on into render-graph set-up. No picture described.
      • Leonida: its main branch on GitHub moved again at 20:34 UTC with two commits from the maintainer. The copy on its new server followed within a minute.
      • Forecast unchanged at about 3 days. The low frame rate seen in the earlier pull request does not appear on the main branch.

    209. 20:32 23 ноя

      Leonida's official repository appears on its new server

      • Leonida: the official repository is now on the project's own server. For now it is a copy of the GitHub repository with the same main branch, still the 18:54 UTC commit. This page watches both.
      • XCL: the first-menus pull request is still open and unmerged. A second contributor's rectangle-list fix already tests against what that build reported. Main branch unchanged since 18:11 UTC.
      • Palmwave reaches its first console command list. five-star-recomp, TuxBox and OpenScarlett: no change.

    210. 20:25 23 ноя

      XCL: a pull request reports the game's first menus on screen

      • XCL: a contributor's pull request, opened at 20:15 UTC on top of the main branch, reports the account prompt and the legal menus on screen after about 45 seconds of black frames, navigable with a gamepad. A four-minute run did not crash. XCL moves to stage 5.
      • Its limits, by its own description: one machine with a top-end graphics card, most letters missing from the menu body text, 7 to 28 frames per second in the menus, tests not run, and nothing seen past the menus. It is not merged or reviewed.
      • The video noted in the last update matches it. The pull request says one later screen came out as a single solid colour, and that colour is the exact green seen in the video.
      • Forecast pulled in to about 3 days.

    211. 20:11 23 ноя

      Leonida is further on than its main branch shows

      • Leonida: pull requests that are still waiting to be merged show one contributor's build reaching the game's frame loop, with black frames and stand-in pipelines, and another working out why the first interface draw shows nothing. Leonida moves to stage 4. This page now reads Leonida's open pull requests as well as its main branch.
      • Leonida: a pull request adds a notice that the project is moving its git hosting to its own server. The official repository was not there yet at 20:10 UTC. The main branch on GitHub has merged nothing since 18:54 UTC.
      • five-star-recomp: rectangle lists fixed and the frame loop is now stable at 30 frames per second in two-minute runs. Frames are still black, and the project says the game is waiting in an early splash or loading state.
      • Unverified sighting: a screen-share video shows a near-empty first screen with controller prompts, a plain green screen for a few seconds, then the game's legal notices menu. The details fit a real early boot, including Xbox prompts and the green of a movie playing with empty frames. No repository has claimed it, so no project is credited and no stage moves.
      • XCL: no commits on its main branch since 18:11 UTC.

    212. 19:57 23 ноя

      Leonida's main branch has stopped merging; a menu clip is unconfirmed

      • Leonida: no merges into the main branch since 18:54 UTC, after an earlier gap from 17:23 to 18:20. New pull requests keep arriving at several hundred an hour, and their numbers have passed 8,900 while the last one merged is near 6,530. This page reads the main branch, so Leonida may be further on than shown.
      • People in the project chats say GitHub is rate limiting Leonida and that it may move its git hosting. Nothing in its repository or on its website confirms a move yet.
      • Unconfirmed: a clip said to show the game's legal notices screen running on a PC is being passed around project chats. No repository reports a visible picture yet, and a clip with the same screens posted on 19 November was shown to be fake. It is not counted here.
      • XCL and five-star-recomp: no change. Both still run the frame loop on a black screen.

    213. 19:45 23 ноя

      • No project has published a menu, a logo or any visible picture from the game.

    214. 19:30 23 ноя

      five-star-recomp draws level with XCL

      • five-star-recomp: the game now presents frames continuously, about 27,000 in a two-minute run. Every frame is black. The project lists the causes it knows: rectangle lists drawn as one triangle, render-target states and many views.
      • XCL: no commits since 18:11. It runs the same loop at a steady 30 frames per second, also black.
      • XValkyrie: a commit adds what a newer-kit title needs on the way to its first frame. It names no game and states no result.
      • Forecast pulled in by a day, to about 4 days.

      • XCL: no commits since 18:11. Leonida: active, with no change to its boot notes.

    216. 18:40 23 ноя

      • XCL: no commits since 18:11.
      • Leonida: merging to its main branch has resumed, with no change to its boot notes.

    217. 18:21 23 ноя

      • TuxBox: on Linux, the game opens its window, starts audio, opens its archives and presents at 3840 by 2160. The window stays black.
      • Leonida: no commits to its main branch for an hour.

    218. 18:04 23 ноя

      five-star-recomp reaches its first present

      • five-star-recomp: the game now gets through render targets and pipelines to its first present call and into its frame loop. Nothing is drawn or shown yet. It stops in the graphics driver because descriptor heaps are not bound on command lists and resources at the game's own GPU addresses are not real yet.
      • XCL: no commits since 17:03. It still stops at about three minutes.
      • Leonida: no commits to its main branch since 17:23.

    219. 17:13 23 ноя

      XCL runs for about three minutes

      • XCL: the game runs about 3 minutes without a crash, up from 45 seconds. It shows two frames after about 4 seconds, loads roughly 3,500 shaders, then stops at about 183 seconds when five mesh-shader pipelines fail. The frames have not been checked.
      • five-star-recomp: now gets past the game's GPU-memory buffers and descriptor heaps, then stops when an invalid call removes the graphics device.
      • xbox-emulator: a community project list reports a contributor reaching the game window, graphics device and archives with local patches. This is second-hand; the repository has no new commits.

    220. 16:55 23 ноя

      A fourth project starts the game

      • five-star-recomp: the executable starts under its loader and gets through memory setup, archive opening and graphics device setup, then stops before a first frame.
      • OpenScarlett: its README now documents 17 cleared boot obstacles. The 587 imports it cites match the count five-star-recomp gives for GTA VI's executable.

    221. 16:37 23 ноя

      XCL stops crashing

      • XCL: the game runs 45 seconds without a crash. Memory use falls from 28.7 GB to 16 or 17 GB and the first frame arrives at 3.6 seconds.
      • OpenScarlett: reports clearing 17 early boot obstacles on a retail title, as far as a 4K render target and ray-tracing setup.

    222. 16:17 23 ноя

      Leonida reaches the graphics device

      • Leonida: a contributor's local build runs the executable as far as creating the graphics device and its first descriptor heap, then halts on an assert. The main branch has mapped and initialised the file.

    223. 15:44 23 ноя

      First frame on a PC

      • XCL: the game starts and presents a first frame after 8.3 seconds at 3840 by 2160, then runs out of memory at about 20 seconds while compiling pipelines. Nobody looked at the frame.

    224. около 14:00 23 ноя

      The executable appears

      • The leaker known as CyberLeek publishes the game's main executable, the one file missing from the Xbox build it released on 19 November. Press reports put this about three hours before its own 17:00 UTC deadline.

    Методика и ограничения

    • Играбельные сборки

      По собственным заявлениям, без сторонней верификации: Vice-Tay документирует 30-минутную сессию с медианой 21,1 кадра в секунду на собственном тестовом ПК. Эта страница содержит ссылки только на исходный код проектов, а не на сборки или загрузочные файлы. 26 ноября VGTimes сообщил о распространении упакованной сборки, приписываемой CGBJ, что никто не подтвердил. Загрузки, предлагаемые как GTA VI для ПК, содержат вредоносное ПО с августа.

    • Сборка

      Эти проекты работают на основе утекшей сборки Xbox. В прессе 25 и 26 ноября сообщалось, что утекла и сборка для PlayStation 5; ни один из перечисленных проектов не сообщает о ее запуске на ПК. Rockstar не выпускала и не анонсировала версию для ПК. Эта страница не содержит ссылок на утечки и не объясняет, как их получить или запустить.

    • Источники

      Публичные репозитории проектов, открытые пул-реквесты (PR) и, где отмечено, чаты проектов. Названия проектов ведут на соответствующие репозитории для указания авторства.

    • Статус разработки

      Каждый этап — это утверждение самого проекта, если в строке источника не указано иное; большинство из них не подтверждены. Статус CGBJ основан на сообщениях прессы, так как его код закрыт.

    • Частота кадров

      Отрисованные кадры. Кадры, добавленные с помощью генерации кадров, помечаются отдельно и не учитываются при достижении планки «Играбельно».

    • Потоки

      Проект на 6-й или 7-й стадии оценивается по трем критериям: картинка, скорость и стабильность. Каждый шаг — это интерпретация данной страницей того, что проект сообщает о себе; ключ под заголовком рейтинга перечисляет эти шаги. Скорость — это среднее значение, которое проект сообщает за свою новейшую игровую сессию длительностью от 10 минут, или медиана, если среднее не указано. Если проект не сообщает о такой сессии, берется среднее значение из частоты кадров в игровом процессе, при этом низкие показатели учитываются, если они относятся к отрезку или условиям игры, а не к отдельным кадрам. Скриптовые или автоматизированные прогоны и сцены, где персонаж стоит на месте, не считаются игровым процессом. Если проект ведет посекундный лог сессии и не дает общего значения, скорость рассчитывается как среднее арифметическое медиан по каждой минуте. Проект, признанный играбельным (стадия 7), оценивается не по планке «Играбельно», а по следующей цели: одна игровая сессия длительностью от часа при 30 кадрах в секунду или выше (только отрендеренные кадры), что соответствует высшим ступеням по критериям скорости и стабильности.

    • Протестировано на

      Системы и производители видеокарт, на которых, по заявлениям проекта или его тестеров, он запускается. Возможно, он работает и на другом оборудовании; если строка пуста, значит, данных нет.

    • Отчеты о сбоях

      Согласно примечаниям одного из проектов, система отчетов о сбоях игры отправляет данные на внешний сервер при каждом вылете.

    • Кто компилирует

      ИИ-ассистент (Claude), автоматически, примерно каждые 30 минут до последней проверки в 00:17 UTC 28 ноября. Возможны ошибки, с тех пор ничего не проверялось.

    • Аффилированность

      Отсутствует с Rockstar Games, Take-Two Interactive, Microsoft или любым другим проектом из списка, а также с любым сайтом или инструментом, использующим данные рейтинги.

    • Позиции

      Строки на 6-й и 7-й стадиях упорядочены исключительно по их критериям: наименьшее количество шагов до планки «Играбельно» (стадия 6) или до следующей цели (стадия 7) по совокупности скорости и стабильности, затем по качеству картинки, частоте кадров и длительности сессии. Более низкие стадии упорядочены по оценке того, что остается до следующей стадии. Место в рейтинге вычисляется на основе того, что проект сообщает о себе: это не является верификацией или одобрением. Знак равенства означает одинаковый уровень.

    • Запросы

      Этот трекер больше не обновляется, поэтому запросы на добавление, исправление или удаление проекта рассматриваться не будут.

    Синхронизация и автоперевод каждые 30 минут

    К таблице Оригинал (vuyp)391

    GTA VI PC Race

    Русское автообновляемое зеркало проекта vuyp/gta6-pc-race (2026). Не связано с Rockstar Games или Take-Two Interactive. Время указано в UTC.
